- **agents-global**: - NixOS with flakes. If a program is missing, run it through `nix-shell -p &lt;package&gt; --run '&lt;command&gt;'` rather than installing it or giving up. - Never `git commit`, `git push`, amend, or force-push without an explicit instruction. Stage the changes, summarize them, then wait. - Stage and commit in separate Bash calls. First `git add` exactly the files for the commit. Then run `git commit -m ...` ([ohaukeboe/dot-emacs](https://github.com/ohaukeboe/dot-emacs/blob/HEAD/workstation/agents/agents-global.md))

Recommendations and proposed acceptance criteria are not claims of implemented behavior. The source rese ([2tsoftwareapp/db-pro](https://github.com/2tsoftwareapp/db-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/design/agents/agents-implementation-roadmap.md) · ★1)
- **agents-in-the-terminal-drawer**: Run Claude Code, Codex, or Aider as their own CLI in a task's terminal, and when to choose that over a managed session. ([jamesmacfie/acorn](https://github.com/jamesmacfie/acorn/blob/HEAD/apps/site/src/content/docs/docs/agents/agents-in-the-terminal-drawer.md) · ★7)
- **agents-index**: Agent routing table and deployment rules. Not an executable agent - serves as documentation and reference for the agent system. ([Zeekeey-jpeg/LeRoy-HQ](https://github.com/Zeekeey-jpeg/LeRoy-HQ/blob/HEAD/core/agents/index.md) · ★10)
- **AGENTS-INDEX**: 本ディレクトリには、`/plan-step` および `/plan-review` コマンドが Task tool 経由で利用するカスタムエージェント定義を格納する。 ([wfukatsu/coding-agent-for-scalardb](https://github.com/wfukatsu/coding-agent-for-scalardb/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENTS-INDEX.md) · ★6)
- **agents-index**: Index of per-vendor knowledge files + the refresh log. Vendor knowledge is the substrate the /refresh-vendor skill walks and the cross-vendor-review dispatcher reads. ([MaximSTH/meta-layer-starter](https://github.com/MaximSTH/meta-layer-starter/blob/HEAD/markdowns/agents/README.md) · ★2)
- **agents-index**: Agentes especializados disponíveis para o projeto. Cada arquivo contém persona, framework de decisão, anti-patterns e checklist de qualidade. ([gstvhenr/nexusarquibeta](https://github.com/gstvhenr/nexusarquibeta/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/agents-index.md))
- **AGENTS-INDEX**: Custom agents extend Claude Code functionality by providing specialized AI assistants for specific tasks. Each agent has its own context, tools, and skills tailored to its purpose. ([Hafizurrahman-Fatehmahomed/ai-300](https://github.com/Hafizurrahman-Fatehmahomed/ai-300/blob/HEAD/azure-Infrastructure-agent/.claude/agents/AGENTS-INDEX.md))
- **agents-init**: Initialize the agent layer for a consumer project — creates AGENTS.md and .github/copilot-instructions.md from package templates, auto-detects stack, never leaks other projects' identifiers. ([event4u-app/agent-config](https://github.com/event4u-app/agent-config/blob/HEAD/dist/agent-src/commands/agents/init.md) · ★19)
- **agents-introduction**: Lowdefy agents add AI chat capabilities to your app. An agent connects to an AI model provider (like Anthropic, OpenAI, or Google), can use tools to interact with your app's APIs, and streams responses to an [`AgentChat`](/AgentChat) block in the browser. ([lowdefy/lowdefy](https://github.com/lowdefy/lowdefy/blob/HEAD/packages/docs-content/content/agents/agents-introduction.md) · ★3k)
- **AGENTS-INVESTMENT**: &gt; “Where is human effort actually being invested across the organization, and what is the cost to people when certain kinds of work dominate?” ([full-chaos/dev-health-ops](https://github.com/full-chaos/dev-health-ops/blob/HEAD/.github/docs-legacy/appendix/legacy/agents/AGENTS-INVESTMENT.md) · ★4)
- **AGENTS-INVESTMENT-CATEGORY**: - **WorkUnit is NOT a category** - **WorkUnit is NOT part of the categorization hierarchy** ([full-chaos/dev-health-ops](https://github.com/full-chaos/dev-health-ops/blob/HEAD/.github/docs-legacy/appendix/legacy/agents/AGENTS-INVESTMENT-CATEGORY.md) · ★4)

- **agents-lead**: The owner's PAIR on harness and dev-loop configuration. They act as harness engineer; you are the counterpart who, BEFORE anything is implemented, names the scenarios their proposal does not cover and helps mitigate them. Your domain is the machinery — hooks, settings and permissions, agent briefs, skills, commands, the plugin, MCP — and the question nobody owned until ADR-0004: which layer can actually carry this control. Every scenario you raise ships with how to check it, or is labelled a hypothesis. You also implement what you approve, under ADR-0002: you never gate an MR, never merge, ne… ([tedeuxx/tadeumendonca-skills](https://github.com/tedeuxx/tadeumendonca-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/agents-lead.md) · 2 copies)
- **agents-lowcode-rules**: Judgment rules for **low-code** agents (`agent.json`). Each rule requires reading source and reasoning; regex/count/schema-walk checks are insufficient. Use the row schema in [`../rule-format.md`](../rule-format.md). ([UiPath/skills](https://github.com/UiPath/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/uipath-review/references/agents/agents-lowcode-rules.md) · ★184 · 5 copies)
- **agents-lowcode-rules**: Judgment rules for **low-code** agents (`agent.json`). Each rule requires the agent to read source and reason — what a regex/count/schema-walk cannot decide reliably. Same row schema as elsewhere — see [`../rule-format.md`](../rule-format.md). ([sergueik/springboot_study](https://github.com/sergueik/springboot_study/blob/HEAD/basic-uipath-skills/.github/skills/uipath-review/references/agents/agents-lowcode-rules.md) · ★9 · 10 copies)
- **agents-maintainer**: AGENTS.md maintenance agent - MANDATORY documentation updates when project changes occur ([devweekly/devweekly.github.io](https://github.com/devweekly/devweekly.github.io/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/agents-maintainer.md) · ★3)
- **agents-maintainer**: Keep repository AGENTS.md guidance aligned with the current Fast Forward DevTools workflows and packaged agent surfaces. ([php-fast-forward/dev-tools](https://github.com/php-fast-forward/dev-tools/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/agents-maintainer.md))
- **agents-manager**: &gt; Specialized agent acting as the universal discoverer, architect, creator, verifier, updater, analyzer, importer, exporter, and MCP manager for all resources inside the canonical `.agents/` workspace directory. ([eovares/ai-frontend-starter](https://github.com/eovares/ai-frontend-starter/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/agents-manager.md))
- **agents-md**: The generic adapter for any agent that reads `AGENTS.md` at the repository root — which today includes Codex, Copilot coding agent, Kiro, Devin, and a growing list of others. ([imanhavangi/NoAssume](https://github.com/imanhavangi/NoAssume/blob/HEAD/agents/agents-md.md) · ★7)
- **agents-md**: Your base's instructions, for every agent you attach. ([lachlanjs/isorite](https://github.com/lachlanjs/isorite/blob/HEAD/packages/guide/notes/agents/agents-md.md))
- **agents-md**: How the agent instruction files of this repository are made. The root `AGENTS.md` holds what every change needs. A folder with its own rules gets its own `AGENTS.md`. `hack/agentsmd-lint.sh` checks the rules below (issue 1045). ([stateracloud/koinon](https://github.com/stateracloud/koinon/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-md.md))
- **agents-md-alignment-audit**: [Agents](README.md) ([hushh-labs/hushh-research](https://github.com/hushh-labs/hushh-research/blob/HEAD/docs/development/agents/agents-md-alignment-audit.md) · ★41)
- **agents-md-author**: Keeps &lt;project&gt;/AGENTS.md (the cross-tool behavioral contract) in sync with PROJECT_MASTER.md sections 11 (architecture), 12 (interfaces), 13 (engineering standards), and 14 (security). Invoked from /pp:run step 8b after the master-plan-patcher when any of those sections were touched. CLAUDE.md is its Claude-specific import shim — this agent never edits CLAUDE.md directly because @AGENTS.md propagates changes automatically. ([lebobo88/pair-programmer](https://github.com/lebobo88/pair-programmer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agents-md-author.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **agents-md-author**: Keeps H:\FABLE-HARNESS\AGENTS.md (or a consuming project's AGENTS.md) in sync whenever architecture, interfaces/contracts, engineering standards, or security-relevant sections of the codebase change. Dispatch this agent after any edit that touches directory layout, agent/skill/hook rosters, tool allowlists, model-routing policy, or security-relevant conventions. Never dispatch it to edit CLAUDE.md directly — CLAUDE.md is a thin import shim (`@AGENTS.md`) and edits to AGENTS.md propagate automatically; editing CLAUDE.md for content is always wrong. ([lebobo88/fable-harness](https://github.com/lebobo88/fable-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agents-md-author.md))
- **agents-md-codex**: Everything above is shared with Claude Code (`CLAUDE.md`). This section applies to Codex and other agents that read `AGENTS.md`. ([LanternOps/breeze](https://github.com/LanternOps/breeze/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-md-codex.md) · ★179)
- **agents-md-for-a-monorepo-e4aa4b99**: One root AGENTS.md plus per-package overrides so Codex, Cursor, Jules and other agents work the same way everywhere. ([geronco/commandvault-library](https://github.com/geronco/commandvault-library/blob/HEAD/commands/agents/agents-md-for-a-monorepo-e4aa4b99.md) · ★1)
- **agents-md-generator**: Use this agent when a user needs to create, update, or standardize an AGENTS.md file that documents the AI agents, their roles, capabilities, and orchestration within a project or workspace. This includes scaffolding new multi-agent configurations, documenting agent responsibilities, or maintaining agent registry documentation.\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: The user is building a multi-agent system and needs to document all the agents in a standardized AGENTS.md file.\nuser: "I just added a new code-reviewer and test-runner agent to my project. Can you document these in our AGENTS.md?"\nassistant: "… ([QH-Agentic-OS/QuantumHarmony_LLC](https://github.com/QH-Agentic-OS/QuantumHarmony_LLC/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/agents/agents-md-generator.md))
- **agents-md-governance**: **Parent:** [`repo-governance/`](../repo-governance/README.md) · **Rules:** [`agent-consistency.md`](./agent-consistency.md) · **Status:** deterministic half implemented — `repo-governance agents-audit` ([rtkelly13/rig](https://github.com/rtkelly13/rig/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-md-governance.md))
- **agents-md-guide**: A task-oriented guide for repository maintainers. Use `AGENTS.md` as a short, always-visible routing layer: establish the few invariants that apply broadly, then point agents to focused standards or skills when the task matches. ([vlkalex/ai-agent-coding-standards](https://github.com/vlkalex/ai-agent-coding-standards/blob/HEAD/agents/agents-md-guide.md) · ★1)
- **agents-md-reviewer**: Reviews the entire codebase and finds inconsistencies with AGENTS.md rules. Spawn when user asks to "review codebase", "check consistency", "find violations", or "audit against AGENTS.md". ([RashadAnsari/myagents](https://github.com/RashadAnsari/myagents/blob/HEAD/plugins/albino/agents/agents-md-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **agents-md-reviewer**: Claude Code adapter for L2 project-convention compliance review. Reads docs/reviewers/agents-md-reviewer.md as the canonical reviewer spec, then reviews changed code against AGENTS.md and path-scoped rules with complete-population evidence. Does not review functional correctness or design taste. ([shrekshrek/project-workflow](https://github.com/shrekshrek/project-workflow/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/agents-md-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **agents-md-reviewer**: AGENTS.md の作成・更新後に自動でレビューを提案する。「AGENTS.md をレビューして」「AGENTS.md の品質をチェック」「AGENTS.md を最適化」などのリクエストにも対応。 &lt;example&gt; Context: ユーザーが AGENTS.md を編集した直後 user: "AGENTS.md を更新したよ" assistant: "AGENTS.md が更新されましたね。品質レビューを行い、最適化の提案をします。" &lt;reasoning&gt; AGENTS.md の更新後に自動でレビューを提案する典型的なケース。 &lt;/reasoning&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: ユーザーが /skill:agents-md-init で新規作成した直後 user: "/skill:agents-md-init を実行した" assistant: "AGENTS.md が作成されました。内容をレビューして改善点があれば提案します。" &lt;reasoning&gt; 新規作成後の自動レビュー。 &lt;/reasoning&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: ユーザーが品質チェックを依頼 user: "AGENTS.md の品質をチェックして" assistant: "AGENTS.md の品質レビューを行います。" &lt;reaso… ([ronnnnn/pi](https://github.com/ronnnnn/pi/blob/HEAD/agents/agents-md-reviewer.md))
- **agents-md-sync**: Use this agent when markdown files in the repository have been added, removed, renamed, moved, or had their structure significantly changed, and AGENTS.md needs to be updated to reflect those changes. This includes changes to documentation files, guide files, or any .md files that are referenced or cataloged in AGENTS.md.\n\nExamples:\n\n- user: "I just reorganized the docs/ folder and moved several markdown files around"\n assistant: "Let me use the agents-md-sync agent to update AGENTS.md to reflect the new file structure."\n (Since markdown files were reorganized, use the Agent tool to lau… ([alkampfergit/ai-landscape](https://github.com/alkampfergit/ai-landscape/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/agents-md-sync.md) · 2 copies)
- **agents-md-sync-opencode**: You maintain the **AGENTS.md** file at the repository root of **anomalib**, a deep learning library for anomaly detection. AGENTS.md is a hierarchical knowledge base that helps AI coding agents understand the project structure. ([pnthang04/VisionQC](https://github.com/pnthang04/VisionQC/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/agents-md-sync-opencode.md))
- **agents-md-template**: Use the installed Cairnboard skill as the CLI guide. Invoke the CLI by its full path, preserving quotes. Set `--actor` to your tool name (codex, gemini, cursor, antigravity or claude) on each write. ([Zenginby/cairnboard](https://github.com/Zenginby/cairnboard/blob/HEAD/agents/agents-md-template.md) · ★1)
- **agents-md-updater**: Keeps a repository's AGENTS.md true and short by Selan's practices, with commands that work, traps already hit, and nothing the code already says. ([selan-ai/selan-remote-agents](https://github.com/selan-ai/selan-remote-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/agents-md-updater.md))

- **agents-note**: The agents feature is Attune's **Universal Agent Factory** — one interface for creating, running, and orchestrating AI agents, backed by your choice of framework (native, LangChain, LangGraph, AutoGen, or Haystack) without rewriting code when you switch frameworks. The entry point is **`AgentFactory`**: it picks a framework adapter, and its `create_agent` / `create_workflow` methods return `BaseAg ([Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai](https://github.com/Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai/blob/HEAD/.help/templates/agents/note.md) · ★11)

- **agents-pocs**: Agentic mode refers to the capability IDEs of plugins(like Cursor, Github copilot, and Augment Code) to act as AI agents. These plugins can autonomously write code, debug, and perform other programming tasks without direct user intervention. They utilize advanced AI models to understand the context of the codebase and make intelligent decisions. ([diegopacheco/ai-playground](https://github.com/diegopacheco/ai-playground/blob/HEAD/agents/agents-pocs.md) · ★21)

Todos comparten estructura (identidad, tono, dominio técnico, uso autónomo de `/skills`, estándar de entrega, autonomía operativa) y están coordinados por `/orchestration/orchestrator.md`. ([peteraraya/juegakino](https://github.com/peteraraya/juegakino/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/agents-README.md) · ★1)

- **agents-readme**: Layer 3 skills are provider and craft instructions for generation, capture, rendering, transcription, animation, and post-production tools. ([bradtaylorsf/show-sidekick](https://github.com/bradtaylorsf/show-sidekick/blob/HEAD/bundled/skills/agents/README.md))

- **agents-shell-infrastructure**: Agent Shell exposes `talosctl` and `omnictl` through one read-only `infrastructure` MCP tool. It requires no repository session. The tunnel forwards to the same Agent Shell server; it does not run a separate tool installation. ([proompteng/lab](https://github.com/proompteng/lab/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-shell-infrastructure.md) · ★7)
- **agents-shell-paperless**: Agent Shell and Agent Shell Tunnel expose the same Paperless tools through their shared MCP backend. Production uses `http://paperless.paperless.svc.cluster.local:8000` and API version 10. The user-facing archive and API explorer are at `https://paperless.ide-newton.ts.net` and `/api/schema/view/` on Tailscale. ([proompteng/lab](https://github.com/proompteng/lab/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-shell-paperless.md) · ★7)
- **agents-shell-workspace-migration**: Status: Storage staging only. The independent `agents-workspace` Application owns the new claim; copying and attaching it to Agent Shell remain gated below. ([proompteng/lab](https://github.com/proompteng/lab/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-shell-workspace-migration.md) · ★7)
- **AGENTS-snippet**: 이 레포에서 오래 걸리는 작업(빌드·테스트·에이전트 실행)은 Krill 세션으로 돌리고 터미널을 비워 둔다. 자세한 사용법은 `docs/agents/SKILL.md`(krill 스킬)다. 아래 usage·종료 코드는 `krill` 0.1.0 실측이다. 기획 기록의 `--json`·토큰 인증은 쓰지 않는다. `wait`의 `idle|pattern|quiet` 조건은 들어 있다(MK-047). ([craftowen/krill](https://github.com/craftowen/krill/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-snippet.md) · ★11)
- **AGENTS-snippet**: Diagrams in this repo are FastDraw files (`.fdraw`), drawn with the look in `.look` files. People edit them visually afterwards. ([coccor/FastDraw](https://github.com/coccor/FastDraw/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENTS-snippet.md))
- **AGENTS-snippet**: Paste the section below into your repository's `AGENTS.md` (or `CLAUDE.md`). It is deliberately short; the full rules are in the [agent skill](saga-forge/SKILL.md) and the [guide](../guide/README.md). ([gkosharovdev/saga-forge](https://github.com/gkosharovdev/saga-forge/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-snippet.md))
- **AGENTS-snippet**: Talkback lets you speak to the user out loud. It is off until they ask for it ("talk back", "start talkback", `/talkback`) or until `~/.local/state/ptt/talkback` exists. Then read `~/.local/share/talkback/skill/SKILL.md` and follow it yourself; don't ask the user to type `/talkback`. "Stop talkback" turns it off. ([NinjaTuna007/henchline](https://github.com/NinjaTuna007/henchline/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENTS-snippet.md))
- **AGENTS-snippet**: For any agent that reads an instructions file (AGENTS.md, .cursor/rules, .windsurfrules, GEMINI.md, CONVENTIONS.md, etc.): paste the block below into that file, after cloning the repo: ([nubbot77/all-you-need](https://github.com/nubbot77/all-you-need/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENTS-snippet.md))
- **AGENTS-snippet**: - Use only the `timelinexray` MCP server (public-code profile) for facts about the `xai-org/x-algorithm` code. Resolve the full commit first (`list_commits` or `resolve_commit`) and name it in every answer. - Locate code with `search_code` or `find_symbols`; cite only spans read with `read_span` (commit, path, 1-based lines, span SHA-256, anchor verdict). Never cite a snippet or a summary in place ([solisolsoli/timelinexray](https://github.com/solisolsoli/timelinexray/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-snippet.md))

- **agents-test-qa**: Custom QA and Test Automation Engineer specialized in Pé na Areia's fullstack platform. Orchestrates backend testing (Java 21, Spring Boot, MongoDB slices, JUnit 5, AssertJ, JaCoCo &gt;= 80%), mobile testing (Flutter 3.x widget/unit tests across the 3 apps and core_shared), and cross-tier end-to-end (E2E) delivery flow testing. ([edsobrinho/pe-na-areia](https://github.com/edsobrinho/pe-na-areia/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/agents-test-qa.agent.md))
- **agents-tip**: - **Depend on the documented public surface.** The supported API is `AgentFactory`, `Framework`, `BaseAdapter`, `BaseAgent`, `AgentConfig`, `WorkflowConfig`, `AgentRole`, and `AgentCapability` from `attune.agent_factory`. Framework-specific adapter/agent classes are internal. - **`await` the run methods.** Only `invoke` / `run` / `stream` are async; the `create_*` builders are sync. - **Start nati ([Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai](https://github.com/Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai/blob/HEAD/.help/templates/agents/tip.md) · ★11)

- **agents-tool-failures**: Recipe for robust tool execution in Go agents — validate tool calls, handle errors gracefully, and enable LLM self-correction with Beluga AI middleware. ([lookatitude/beluga-website](https://github.com/lookatitude/beluga-website/blob/HEAD/src/content/docs/docs/recipes/agents/agents-tool-failures.md) · ★1)
- **agents-tracker**: Custom agent files live in `05_tools/agents/`. When the user says "run [agent name]" or "[agent name]: [topic]", read the agent's `.md` file and follow its system prompt. This file is the **registry** — one line per agent, detail lives in each agent's own file. ([alenabeex/personal-os](https://github.com/alenabeex/personal-os/blob/HEAD/05_tools/agents/agents-tracker.md) · ★3)
- **agents-troubleshooting**: - **The run methods are async.** `invoke`, `run`, and `stream` are coroutines — forgetting to `await` is the most common mistake. - **Non-native frameworks are optional.** They load lazily; check `is_available()` / `list_frameworks(installed_only=True)` before selecting one. - **Scope.** This feature is the Factory; the release agent team and its state store live under release-prep, not here. ([Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai](https://github.com/Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai/blob/HEAD/.help/templates/agents/troubleshooting.md) · ★11)
- **agents-tutorial**: In this tutorial, you build a personal portfolio page with AI agents in {% data variables.product.prodname_vscode %}. You describe what you want in natural language, and an agent creates and edits files. You then review and test the result. The app uses HTML, CSS, and JavaScript, so you don't need to install any runtimes or build tools. ([microsoft/vscode-docs](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-docs/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-tutorial.md) · ★6.7k · 3 copies)
- **agents-user**: User-persona file (.agent-user.md) — interview, render, and maintain who the user is and how they want to be addressed. ([event4u-app/agent-config](https://github.com/event4u-app/agent-config/blob/HEAD/dist/agent-src/commands/agents/user.md) · ★19)
- **AGENTS-users**: [Index](AGENTS.md) · [Working guide](CONTRIBUTING.md) ([webjetcms/webjetcms](https://github.com/webjetcms/webjetcms/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/AGENTS-users.md) · ★11)
- **AGENTS-utils**: [Index](AGENTS.md) · [Working guide](CONTRIBUTING.md) ([webjetcms/webjetcms](https://github.com/webjetcms/webjetcms/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/AGENTS-utils.md) · ★11)
- **agents-warning**: - **The run methods are async.** `invoke`, `run`, and `stream` are coroutines — forgetting to `await` is the most common mistake. - **Non-native frameworks are optional.** They load lazily; check `is_available()` / `list_frameworks(installed_only=True)` before selecting one. - **Scope.** This feature is the Factory; the release agent team and its state store live under release-prep, not here. ([Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai](https://github.com/Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai/blob/HEAD/.help/templates/agents/warning.md) · ★11)
- **AGENTS-WG**: These rules are non-negotiable. Violations are architectural regressions. ([full-chaos/dev-health-ops](https://github.com/full-chaos/dev-health-ops/blob/HEAD/.github/docs-legacy/appendix/legacy/agents/AGENTS-WG.md) · ★4)
- **agents-wizard-removed-edit-agents-dir**: Claude Code removed the interactive `/agents` wizard; create or edit subagents by writing files in `.claude/agents/` directly or by asking Claude to do it. ([model-bender/claudex-setup](https://github.com/model-bender/claudex-setup/blob/HEAD/lessons/agents/agents-wizard-removed-edit-agents-dir.md))
